// ROUND 6B: the structural corners the round-5 sweeps left. Four rules
// measured here — the identity of an INDEXING fold, the type a BOOLEAN fold
// keeps, which radix kinds DECODE will read, and the order two empty sides
// settle a JOIN's type by.

// Indexing and permuting leave their right argument alone when the left
// one names every item in order, so `i.` of the cell's own shape is the
// identity of both.

$ ({)/ (0 $ 0)

// A fold of BOOLEANS whose operation cannot leave `0 1` keeps the
// BOOLEAN type, where a sum or a difference widens to an integer.

// DECODE and the kind of its radices. A radix that weighs nothing is
// refused wherever the reference's own path READS it: a character radix
// refuses booleans, a boxed one booleans and integers, a symbol one every
// digit, and none of them refuses a float, an exact number or a complex one.
// A radix list with nothing in it weighs nothing at all, and the zero it
// answers with is the running total's own — a float for ordinary numbers, the
// integer for booleans, the boolean where either side is exact and the complex
// where either is complex.

// A JOIN between two sides that share no type settles it by the
// reference's order over the nine — a boolean loses to everything, then a
// character, a whole number, a box, an extended number, a rational, a float, a
// complex number, and a symbol last — and a side with NOTHING IN IT names no
// type at all, not even where the two would have promoted. Laminate, stitch
// and a ranked catenation follow the same order catenation does.

// A FETCH path of more than one box descends a step at a time, and only a
// BOX is descended into: a value no box held takes another step only while it
// is an atom. An empty step selects the level whole and opens a boxed SCALAR
// alone, and a step that is itself a boxed scalar holds its indices one box
// deeper.
(a:) {:: (1 2 3)
$ (a:) {:: (1 2 3)
(a:) {:: (,5)
$ (a:) {:: (,5)
(a:) {:: 5
$ (a:) {:: 5

// and the obverse composes the other way round — `v^:_1 @ u^:_1` — taking
// the rank of `u^:_1` with it rather than the one the original carried.
